<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> side</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">max</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">floor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(visibleCount </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> angle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 90</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (side </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> radius</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> itemHeight </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">tan</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((angle </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> DEG</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> height</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> radius </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">sin</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(side </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> angle </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> DEG</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> itemHeight)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }, [visibleCount, itemHeight])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
